
跑完后记住结果，然后：

**第二步：停止 fp8 server，等 GPU 释放**

```bash
kill $(pgrep -f "sglang.launch_server")
sleep 15
nvidia-smi | grep MiB   # 确认显存已释放（应该 <3000 MiB）
```

**第三步：启动 bf16 server**

```bash
cd /root/shared-nvme/openbmb
bash bench/start_server_bf16.sh > bench/outputs/server_bf16.log 2>&1 &
```

**第四步：等 server 就绪（约 90-120 秒，手动轮询）**

```bash
watch -n 3 'curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:30000/health && echo "READY" || echo "not ready"'
```

看到 `READY` 后 Ctrl+C，然后：

**第五步：跑 bf16 bench**

```bash
cd /root/shared-nvme/openbmb

export SPEED_DATA_S1=bench/data/speed_bench_c1.jsonl
export SPEED_DATA_S8=bench/data/speed_bench_c8.jsonl
export SPEED_DATA_SMAX=bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl

bash toolkit/bench_serving.sh http://127.0.0.1:30000 2>&1 | tee bench/outputs/bench_bf16.log
```

**第六步：对比（两个 log 跑完后执行）**

```bash
python3 - << 'EOF'
import re

def parse(f):
    txt = open(f).read()
    res = {}
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r'\s+(S1|S8|Smax)\s*:\s*([0-9.]+)', line)
        if m:
            res[m.group(1)] = float(m.group(2))
    return res

fp8  = parse("bench/outputs/bench_fp8.log")
bf16 = parse("bench/outputs/bench_bf16.log")

print(f"\n{'档位':>5}  {'FP8(s)':>10}  {'BF16(s)':>10}  {'加速比':>8}")
print("-" * 42)
for k in ["S1", "S8", "Smax"]:
    f = fp8.get(k, 0)
    b = bf16.get(k, 0)
    if f and b:
        print(f"  {k:>3}  {f:>10.1f}  {b:>10.1f}  {b/f:>7.3f}x")
EOF
```

```bash
import re
```

> AGENT

`bench_serving.py` 本身有 tqdm，问题是外面套了两层 shell 把输出弄乱了。写一个干净的单文件 Python 工具替换全部 shell 脚本：

> TOOL

tool_use edit_file_v2
```json
{
  "relativeWorkspacePath": "/root/shared-nvme/openbmb/bench/bench.py",
  "streamingContent": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nbench/bench.py — 本地速度基准测试\n\n用法:\n  python3 bench/bench.py [--tier S1|S8|Smax|all] [--api URL] [--out FILE]\n\n示例:\n  python3 bench/bench.py --tier S8\n  python3 bench/bench.py --tier all --out bench/outputs/run1.json\n\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport time\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nSOAR_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nDATA_DIR   = SOAR_ROOT / \"bench\" / \"data\"\nOUT_DIR    = SOAR_ROOT / \"bench\" / \"outputs\"\nPYTHON     = \"/root/shared-nvme/conda/envs/soar/bin/python3\"\nPYTHONPATH = \":\".join([\n    str(SOAR_ROOT / \"demo-sala\" / \"sglang\" / \"python\"),\n    str(SOAR_ROOT / \"kernels\" / \"infllmv2_cuda_impl\"),\n])\n\nTIERS = {\n    \"S1\":   {\"file\": \"speed_bench_c1.jsonl\",          \"concurrency\": 1},\n    \"S8\":   {\"file\": \"speed_bench_c8.jsonl\",          \"concurrency\": 8},\n    \"Smax\": {\"file\": \"speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl\",  \"concurrency\": None},\n}\n# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\ndef convert_data(src: Path, dst: Path) -> int:\n    \"\"\"将 {question, model_response} JSONL 转为 bench_serving custom 格式。\"\"\"\n    n = 0\n    with open(src, encoding=\"utf-8\") as fin, open(dst, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fout:\n        for line in fin:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            item = json.loads(line)\n            prompt = item.get(\"question\") or item.get(\"input\") or item.get(\"prompt\")\n            if prompt is None:\n                raise KeyError(f\"找不到 prompt 字段: {list(item.keys())}\")\n            fout.write(json.dumps({\n                \"conversations\": [\n                    {\"role\": \"user\",      \"content\": prompt},\n                    {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": item.get(\"model_response\", \"\")},\n                ]\n            }, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n            n += 1\n    return n\n\n\ndef run_tier(tier: str, api: str, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"跑单档 bench，实时输出（含 tqdm），返回 {duration, throughput, ...}。\"\"\"\n    cfg  = TIERS[tier]\n    src  = DATA_DIR / cfg[\"file\"]\n    conc = cfg[\"concurrency\"]\n\n    if not src.exists():\n        print(f\"  [SKIP] 数据文件不存在: {src}\")\n        return {\"tier\": tier, \"duration\": None}\n\n    host = api.split(\"//\")[1].split(\":\")[0]\n    port = api.split(\":\")[-1].rstrip(\"/\")\n\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, mode=\"w\") as tmp:\n        tmp_path = tmp.name\n\n    try:\n        n = convert_data(src, Path(tmp_path))\n        print(f\"\\n  [{tier}] {n} 条请求  并发={'unlimited' if conc is None else conc}\")\n\n        cmd = [\n            PYTHON, \"-m\", \"sglang.bench_serving\",\n            \"--backend\",      \"sglang\",\n            \"--host\",         host,\n            \"--port\",         port,\n            \"--dataset-name\", \"custom\",\n            \"--dataset-path\", tmp_path,\n            \"--num-prompts\",  str(n),\n            \"--flush-cache\",\n        ]\n        if conc is not None:\n            cmd += [\"--max-concurrency\", str(conc)]\n\n        env = {**os.environ, \"PYTHONPATH\": PYTHONPATH, \"PYTHONUNBUFFERED\": \"1\"}\n\n        t0 = time.time()\n        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, env=env, text=True, capture_output=False)\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n\n        # bench_serving 把 summary 打到 stdout，上面已实时输出了\n        # 返回耗时供汇总用（duration 从 bench_serving 内部 tqdm 后打印的行里提取）\n        return {\"tier\": tier, \"elapsed_wall\": elapsed, \"exit_code\": proc.returncode}\n\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(tmp_path)\n\n\ndef wait_server(api: str, timeout: int = 300) -> bool:\n    import urllib.request\n    url = api.rstrip(\"/\") + \"/health\"\n    print(f\"[wait] 检查 server {url} ...\")\n    for i in range(1, timeout + 1):\n        try:\n            urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=2)\n            print(f\"[wait] Server 就绪 ({i}s)\")\n            return True\n        except Exception:\n            if i % 10 == 0:\n                print(f\"[wait] 等待中... {i}s / {timeout}s\")\n            time.sleep(1)\n    print(f\"[ERROR] Server {timeout}s 内未就绪\")\n    return False\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"SOAR bench 工具\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--tier\",    default=\"S8\", choices=[\"S1\", \"S8\", \"Smax\", \"all\"],\n                    help=\"评测档位（默认 S8）\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--api\",     default=\"http://127.0.0.1:30000\",\n                    help=\"SGLang API 地址\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--wait\",    action=\"store_true\",\n                    help=\"等待 server 就绪后再开始\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--out\",     default=None,\n                    help=\"将结果 JSON 写入此文件\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--no-verbose\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    tiers = list(TIERS.keys()) if args.tier == \"all\" else [args.tier]\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  SOAR Bench  |  {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\")\n    print(f\"  API: {args.api}  |  档位: {', '.join(tiers)}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    if args.wait:\n        if not wait_server(args.api):\n            sys.exit(1)\n\n    results = []\n    for tier in tiers:\n        r = run_tier(tier, args.api, verbose=not args.no_verbose)\n        results.append(r)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  汇总\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    for r in results:\n        dur = f\"{r.get('elapsed_wall', 0):.1f}s (wall)\" if r.get(\"elapsed_wall\") else \"SKIP\"\n        print(f\"  {r['tier']:>5}: {dur}\")\n\n    if args.out:\n        OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        out = args.out if os.path.isabs(args.out) else str(SOAR_ROOT / args.out)\n        with open(out, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump({\"timestamp\": datetime.now().isoformat(),\n                       \"api\": args.api, \"results\": results}, f, indent=2)\n        print(f\"\\n  结果写入: {out}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n",
  "noCodeblock": true,
  "cloudAgentEdit": false
}
```

**用法（server 已就绪直接跑）：**

```bash
# 只跑 S8
python3 bench/bench.py --tier S8

# 等 server 就绪再跑（启动后立即执行）
python3 bench/bench.py --tier S8 --wait

# 全部三档
python3 bench/bench.py --tier all

# 结果保存到文件
python3 bench/bench.py --tier S8 --out bench/outputs/fp8_s8.json
```

```json
{
  "relativeWorkspacePath": "/root/shared-nvme/openbmb/bench/bench.py",
  "streamingContent": "def convert_data(src: Path, dst: Path, sample: int = None, seed: int = 42) -> int:\n    \"\"\"将 {question, model_response} JSONL 转为 bench_serving custom 格式。\n    \n    sample: 随机抽取条数（None = 全部）\n    \"\"\"\n    import random\n    rows = []\n    with open(src, encoding=\"utf-8\") as fin:\n        for line in fin:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            item = json.loads(line)\n            prompt = item.get(\"question\") or item.get(\"input\") or item.get(\"prompt\")\n            if prompt is None:\n                raise KeyError(f\"找不到 prompt 字段: {list(item.keys())}\")\n            rows.append({\n                \"conversations\": [\n                    {\"role\": \"user\",      \"content\": prompt},\n                    {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": item.get(\"model_response\", \"\")},\n                ]\n            })\n\n    if sample is not None and sample < len(rows):\n        random.seed(seed)\n        rows = random.sample(rows, sample)\n        print(f\"  [sample] 随机抽取 {sample}/{len(rows) + sample} 条（seed={seed}）\")\n\n    with open(dst, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fout:\n        for row in rows:\n            fout.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    return len(rows)",

}
```

```json
{
  "relativeWorkspacePath": "/root/shared-nvme/openbmb/bench/bench.py",
  "streamingContent": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport json, os, random, subprocess, sys, tempfile, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# ── 配置区（直接改这里）──────────────────────────────────\nAPI    = \"http://127.0.0.1:30000\"\nTIER   = \"S8\"        # S1 / S8 / Smax / all\nSAMPLE = 8           # None = 全部\nSEED   = 42\n# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nROOT   = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nDATA   = ROOT / \"bench/data\"\nPYTHON = \"/root/shared-nvme/conda/envs/soar/bin/python3\"\nPYTHONPATH = \":\".join([\n    str(ROOT / \"demo-sala/sglang/python\"),\n    str(ROOT / \"kernels/infllmv2_cuda_impl\"),\n])\n\nTIERS = {\n    \"S1\":   (\"speed_bench_c1.jsonl\",         1),\n    \"S8\":   (\"speed_bench_c8.jsonl\",         8),\n    \"Smax\": (\"speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl\", None),\n}\n\n\ndef convert(src, sample, seed):\n    rows = []\n    for line in Path(src).read_text().splitlines():\n        if not line.strip():\n            continue\n        d = json.loads(line)\n        prompt = d.get(\"question\") or d.get(\"input\") or d.get(\"prompt\")\n        rows.append({\"conversations\": [\n            {\"role\": \"user\",      \"content\": prompt},\n            {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": d.get(\"model_response\", \"\")},\n        ]})\n    if sample and sample < len(rows):\n        random.seed(seed)\n        rows = random.sample(rows, sample)\n    tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=\".jsonl\", delete=False, mode=\"w\")\n    for r in rows:\n        tmp.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    tmp.close()\n    return tmp.name, len(rows)\n\n\ndef run(tier):\n    fname, conc = TIERS[tier]\n    src = DATA / fname\n    if not src.exists():\n        print(f\"[skip] {src} 不存在\"); return\n\n    host = API.split(\"//\")[1].split(\":\")[0]\n    port = API.split(\":\")[-1].rstrip(\"/\")\n\n    tmp, n = convert(src, SAMPLE, SEED)\n    print(f\"\\n[{tier}] {n} 条  并发={'∞' if conc is None else conc}\")\n    try:\n        cmd = [PYTHON, \"-m\", \"sglang.bench_serving\",\n               \"--backend\", \"sglang\", \"--host\", host, \"--port\", port,\n               \"--dataset-name\", \"custom\", \"--dataset-path\", tmp,\n               \"--num-prompts\", str(n), \"--flush-cache\"]\n        if conc:\n            cmd += [\"--max-concurrency\", str(conc)]\n        subprocess.run(cmd, env={**os.environ, \"PYTHONPATH\": PYTHONPATH,\n                                 \"PYTHONUNBUFFERED\": \"1\"})\n    finally:\n        os.unlink(tmp)\n\n\ntiers = list(TIERS) if TIER == \"all\" else [TIER]\nfor t in tiers:\n    run(t)\n",
  "noCodeblock": true,
  "cloudAgentEdit": false
}
```

> AGENT

问题在于子进程启动时 tqdm 会检测 TTY，`subprocess.run` 会让它认为不在终端里。用 `os.execve` 直接替换当前进程，彻底解决：

> TOOL

tool_use edit_file_v2
```json
{
  "relativeWorkspacePath": "/root/shared-nvme/openbmb/bench/bench.py",
  "streamingContent": "def run(tier):\n    fname, conc = TIERS[tier]\n    src = DATA / fname\n    if not src.exists():\n        print(f\"[skip] {src} 不存在\"); return\n\n    host = API.split(\"//\")[1].split(\":\")[0]\n    port = API.split(\":\")[-1].rstrip(\"/\")\n\n    tmp, n = convert(src, SAMPLE, SEED)\n    print(f\"\\n[{tier}] {n} 条  并发={'∞' if conc is None else conc}\")\n\n    cmd = [PYTHON, \"-m\", \"sglang.bench_serving\",\n           \"--backend\", \"sglang\", \"--host\", host, \"--port\", port,\n           \"--dataset-name\", \"custom\", \"--dataset-path\", tmp,\n           \"--num-prompts\", str(n), \"--flush-cache\"]\n    if conc:\n        cmd += [\"--max-concurrency\", str(conc)]\n\n    import atexit\n    atexit.register(lambda: Path(tmp).unlink(missing_ok=True))\n\n    env = {**os.environ, \"PYTHONPATH\": PYTHONPATH, \"PYTHONUNBUFFERED\": \"1\"}\n    os.execve(PYTHON, cmd, env)   # 替换当前进程 → tqdm 直连终端\n\n\ntiers = list(TIERS) if TIER == \"all\" else [TIER]",
  "noCodeblock": true,
  "cloudAgentEdit": false
}
```

> TOOL

tool_use edit_file_v2
```json
{
  "relativeWorkspacePath": "/root/shared-nvme/openbmb/bench/bench.py",
  "streamingContent": "tiers = list(TIERS) if TIER == \"all\" else [TIER]\nif len(tiers) == 1:\n    run(tiers[0])\nelse:\n    # 多档时无法 execve，退回 subprocess\n    for t in tiers:\n        fname, conc = TIERS[t]\n        src = DATA / fname\n        if not src.exists():\n            print(f\"[skip] {src}\"); continue\n        host = API.split(\"//\")[1].split(\":\")[0]\n        port = API.split(\":\")[-1].rstrip(\"/\")\n        tmp, n = convert(src, SAMPLE, SEED)\n        print(f\"\\n[{t}] {n} 条  并发={'∞' if conc is None else conc}\")\n        cmd = [PYTHON, \"-m\", \"sglang.bench_serving\",\n               \"--backend\", \"sglang\", \"--host\", host, \"--port\", port,\n               \"--dataset-name\", \"custom\", \"--dataset-path\", tmp,\n               \"--num-prompts\", str(n), \"--flush-cache\"]\n        if conc:\n            cmd += [\"--max-concurrency\", str(conc)]\n        env = {**os.environ, \"PYTHONPATH\": PYTHONPATH, \"PYTHONUNBUFFERED\": \"1\",\n               \"FORCE_COLOR\": \"1\"}\n        try:\n            subprocess.run(cmd, env=env)\n        finally:\n            os.unlink(tmp)",
  "noCodeblock": true,
  "cloudAgentEdit": false
}
```

> AGENT

现在的逻辑：

- **单档**（`S1 / S8 / Smax`）：`os.execve` 直接替换当前进程 → tqdm 100% 直连终端，进度条正常
- **`all`**：退回 subprocess + `FORCE_COLOR=1`，tqdm 也会显示颜色进度
